```python
    workbook.save(copy)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="header"):
        read_founder_workbook(copy)


def _pass(letter, rows):
    from ucc_ml.contracts import PASS_LABELLERS
    from ucc_ml.labeling import PASS_COLUMNS

    records = [{
        "queue_case_id": "e" * 64 if repeat else case_id, "case_id": case_id, "is_repeat": repeat, "part": 1,
        "label": label, "reason_code": code, "reason": f"pass {letter}: {code}", "labeller_id": PASS_LABELLERS[letter],
        "labelled_at": f"2026-09-12T10:00:0{1 if letter == 'a' else 2}Z", "sampling_stratum": "CO:accepted",
        "inclusion_probability": 0.5, "labelling_round": "pilot_v1",
    } for case_id, label, code, repeat in rows]
    return pd.DataFrame(records, columns=list(PASS_COLUMNS))


K = ["1" * 64, "2" * 64, "3" * 64, "4" * 64]
PASS_A = _pass("a", [(K[0], "RELEVANT", "R_BORROWER_EQUIPMENT_WORD", False), (K[1], "RELEVANT", "R_BORROWER_TRADE_WORD", False),
                     (K[2], "RELEVANT", "R_LENDER_NAMED_MAKER", False), (K[3], "RELEVANT", "R_BORROWER_TRADE_WORD", False),
                     (K[0], "RELEVANT", "R_BORROWER_EQUIPMENT_WORD", True)])
PASS_B = _pass("b", [(K[0], "RELEVANT", "R_BORROWER_EQUIPMENT_WORD", False), (K[1], "RELEVANT", "R_BORROWER_TRADE_WORD", False),
                     (K[2], "RELEVANT", "R_LENDER_NAMED_MAKER", False), (K[3], "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE", "I_AMBIGUOUS_WORD", False),
                     (K[0], "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE", "I_GENERIC_NAME", True)])


def _decisions(rows):
    from ucc_ml.labeling import FOUNDER_DECISION_COLUMNS

    return pd.DataFrame([dict(zip(FOUNDER_DECISION_COLUMNS, r)) for r in rows], columns=list(FOUNDER_DECISION_COLUMNS))


DECISIONS = _decisions([
    (K[1], "audit", "RELEVANT", "", "confirmed", "2026-09-13T09:00:00Z"),
    (K[2], "audit", "NOT_RELEVANT", "a law office, not a Terex customer", "overturned", "2026-09-13T09:00:00Z"),
    (K[3], "disagreement", "RELEVANT", "CONCRETE is a trade word", "adjudicated", "2026-09-13T09:00:00Z"),
])


def test_assemble_round_labels_produces_the_four_k3_statuses():
    from ucc_ml.contracts import LABEL_COLUMNS, Label
    from ucc_ml.labeling import assemble_round_labels

    labels = assemble_round_labels(PASS_A, PASS_B, DECISIONS, "pilot_v1", "label_policy_v1")
    assert list(labels.columns) == list(LABEL_COLUMNS) and len(labels) == 6
    originals = labels[~labels.is_repeat].set_index("case_id")
    assert originals.adjudication_status.to_dict() == {K[0]: "model_agreed", K[1]: "founder_confirmed",
                                                       K[2]: "founder_adjudicated", K[3]: "founder_adjudicated"}
    assert originals.at[K[0], "labeller_id"] == "claude_blind_pass_a+claude_blind_pass_b"
    assert originals.at[K[0], "labelled_at"] == "2026-09-12T10:00:02Z" and originals.at[K[0], "reason"] == "pass a: R_BORROWER_EQUIPMENT_WORD"
    assert originals.at[K[1], "labeller_id"] == "founder" and originals.at[K[1], "reason_code"] == "R_BORROWER_TRADE_WORD"
    assert originals.loc[K[2], ["label", "reason_code", "reason"]].tolist() == ["NOT_RELEVANT", "ADJUDICATED", "a law office, not a Terex customer"]
    assert originals.at[K[3], "label"] == "RELEVANT" and originals.at[K[3], "reason_code"] == "ADJUDICATED"
    repeats = labels[labels.is_repeat]
    assert repeats.labeller_id.tolist() == ["claude_blind_pass_a", "claude_blind_pass_b"]
    assert set(repeats.adjudication_status) == {"blind_repeat"} and set(repeats.case_id) == {K[0]}
    assert set(labels.labelling_round) == {"pilot_v1"} and set(labels.policy_version) == {"label_policy_v1"}
    for record in labels.to_dict("records"):
        Label.model_validate(record)


def test_assemble_round_labels_refuses_an_undecided_disagreement():
    from ucc_ml.labeling import UndecidedDisagreements, assemble_round_labels

    with pytest.raises(UndecidedDisagreements, match="1 disagreement") as exc:
        assemble_round_labels(PASS_A, PASS_B, DECISIONS.iloc[:2], "pilot_v1", "label_policy_v1")
    assert exc.value.case_ids == [K[3]] and exc.value.round_name == "pilot_v1"
    wrong = _decisions([(K[0], "disagreement", "RELEVANT", "x", "adjudicated", "2026-09-13T09:00:00Z")])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="but the passes agree"):
        assemble_round_labels(PASS_A, PASS_B, pd.concat([DECISIONS, wrong], ignore_index=True), "pilot_v1", "label_policy_v1")


def test_cli_import_founder_review_complete_and_incomplete(tmp_path, capsys):
    import hashlib

    from ucc_ml.config import load_config
    from ucc_ml.labeling import read_founder_decisions, round_paths

    cfg = reviewed_pilot_repo(tmp_path / "complete")
    rp = round_paths(load_config(cfg), "pilot_v1")
    decisions = read_founder_decisions(rp.founder_decisions)

    lines = rp.founder_digest.read_text().splitlines()
    assert [line.split("  ", 1)[1] for line in lines[:3]] == [

    assert lines[2].split("  ", 1)[0] == hashlib.sha256(rp.founder_decisions.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert "disagreements: 3/3 decided" in capsys.readouterr().out
    reviewed_pilot_repo(tmp_path / "incomplete", decide_all=False)
    assert "INCOMPLETE: 1 disagreement(s)" in capsys.readouterr().out
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Append the founder-import / label-assembly section to `labeling.py`**

Append to `ml/src/ucc_ml/labeling.py`:

```python


# ============================================================================= Task 18
# Founder review import and the K3 assembly of one round's labels.
from ucc_ml.contracts import FOUNDER_REASON_CODE, LABEL_COLUMNS, LABELLER_AGREED, LABELLER_FOUNDER, Label  # noqa: E402

FOUNDER_DECISION_COLUMNS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "case_id", "review_type", "founder_label", "founder_note", "decision", "reviewed_at",
)
FOUNDER_DECISIONS: tuple[str, ...] = ("adjudicated", "confirmed", "overturned")


class UndecidedDisagreements(ValueError):
    """A round still has disagreements between the two blind passes that the founder has not decided."""

    def __init__(self, round_name: str, case_ids: list[str]):
        self.round_name = round_name
        self.case_ids = sorted(case_ids)
        super().__init__(f"{round_name}: {len(self.case_ids)} disagreement(s) have no founder decision, "
                         f"e.g. {self.case_ids[:3]}")


def read_founder_workbook(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """The REVIEW THESE sheet as strings ('' for an empty cell); refuses a missing sheet or a changed header."""
    from openpyxl import load_workbook

    path = Path(path)
    workbook = load_workbook(path, read_only=True, data_only=True)
    try:
        if WORKBOOK_SHEET not in workbook.sheetnames:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: no sheet named {WORKBOOK_SHEET!r}")
        rows = list(workbook[WORKBOOK_SHEET].iter_rows(values_only=True))
    finally:
        workbook.close()
    width = len(WORKBOOK_COLUMNS)
    header = tuple("" if v is None else str(v).strip() for v in (list(rows[0]) if rows else []) + [None] * width)[:width]
    if header != WORKBOOK_COLUMNS:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: header {list(header)} != {list(WORKBOOK_COLUMNS)}")
    records = []
    for values in rows[1:]:
        cells = ["" if v is None else str(v).strip() for v in (list(values) + [None] * width)[:width]]
        if any(cells):
            records.append(cells)
    return pd.DataFrame(records, columns=list(WORKBOOK_COLUMNS), dtype=object)


def founder_summary(decisions: pd.DataFrame, review_manifest: dict) -> dict:
    kinds = Counter(decisions.decision)
    n_disagreements = len(review_manifest["disagreements"])
    audited = kinds["confirmed"] + kinds["overturned"]
    return {
        "disagreements": {"n": n_disagreements, "decided": int(kinds["adjudicated"]),
                          "undecided": int(n_disagreements - kinds["adjudicated"])},
        "audit": {"n_selected": len(review_manifest["audit"]), "n_audited": int(audited),
                  "n_confirmed": int(kinds["confirmed"]), "n_overturned": int(kinds["overturned"]),
                  "agreement_rate": finite_or_none(kinds["confirmed"] / audited) if audited else None},
    }


def import_founder_review(workbook_rows: pd.DataFrame, review_manifest: dict, pass_a: pd.DataFrame,
                          pass_b: pd.DataFrame, reviewed_at: str) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    """The founder's answers as FOUNDER_DECISION_COLUMNS rows, plus founder_summary.

    disagreement row, label + note          -> adjudicated
    audit row, the agreed label             -> confirmed (note optional)
    audit row, a different label + note     -> overturned
    blank founder_label                     -> no decision (an undecided disagreement or an unreviewed audit row)"""
    disagreements, audit = set(review_manifest["disagreements"]), set(review_manifest["audit"])
    if disagreements & audit:
        raise ValueError("the review manifest lists a case both as a disagreement and as an audit row")
    ids = workbook_rows.case_id.tolist()
    duplicated = sorted(c for c, n in Counter(ids).items() if n > 1)
    if duplicated:
        raise ValueError(f"the founder workbook repeats case_id(s) {duplicated[:3]}")
    issued = disagreements | audit
    missing, unexpected = sorted(issued - set(ids)), sorted(set(ids) - issued)
    if missing or unexpected:
        raise ValueError(f"the founder workbook's rows differ from the issued review: "
                         f"missing {missing[:3]}, unexpected {unexpected[:3]}")
    a, b = _originals(pass_a), _originals(pass_b)
    decisions, problems = [], []
    for row in workbook_rows.itertuples(index=False):
        label = row.founder_label.strip().upper()
        note = " ".join(row.founder_note.split())
        if not label:
            continue
        if label not in LABELS:
            problems.append(f"row {row.row}: founder_label {row.founder_label!r} is not one of {list(LABELS)}")
            continue
        passes_agree = a.at[row.case_id, "label"] == b.at[row.case_id, "label"]
        if row.case_id in disagreements:
            review_type, decision = "disagreement", "adjudicated"
            if passes_agree:
                problems.append(f"row {row.row}: listed as a disagreement but the passes agree")
            if not note:
                problems.append(f"row {row.row}: a disagreement decision needs a founder_note")
        else:
            review_type = "audit"
            agreed_label = a.at[row.case_id, "label"]
            if not passes_agree:
                problems.append(f"row {row.row}: listed as an audit row but the passes disagree")
            decision = "confirmed" if label == agreed_label else "overturned"
            if decision == "overturned" and not note:
                problems.append(f"row {row.row}: overturning the agreed label {agreed_label} needs a founder_note")
        decisions.append({"case_id": row.case_id, "review_type": review_type, "founder_label": label,
                          "founder_note": note, "decision": decision, "reviewed_at": reviewed_at})
    if problems:
        raise ValueError("; ".join(problems[:20]))
    frame = pd.DataFrame(decisions, columns=list(FOUNDER_DECISION_COLUMNS))
    return frame, founder_summary(frame, review_manifest)


def read_founder_decisions(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    path = Path(path)
    df = read_exact_csv(path, FOUNDER_DECISION_COLUMNS)
    check_column(path, df, "case_id", is_sha256_hex)
    check_column(path, df, "review_type", lambda v: v in ("disagreement", "audit"))
    check_column(path, df, "founder_label", lambda v: v in LABELS)
    check_column(path, df, "decision", lambda v: v in FOUNDER_DECISIONS)
    return df


def assemble_round_labels(pass_a: pd.DataFrame, pass_b: pd.DataFrame, decisions: pd.DataFrame, round_name: str,
                          policy_version: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """One round's labels.csv rows under contract K3, each validated with contracts.Label.

    passes agree, no founder decision         -> model_agreed (pass A's reason, claude_blind_pass_a+claude_blind_pass_b)
    passes agree, founder confirmed           -> founder_confirmed (pass A's reason, founder)
    passes agree, founder overturned          -> founder_adjudicated (ADJUDICATED, the founder's note)
    passes disagree, founder adjudicated      -> founder_adjudicated (ADJUDICATED, the founder's note)
    passes disagree, no founder decision      -> UndecidedDisagreements; nothing is returned
    every hidden repeat, in each pass         -> blind_repeat (that pass's answer and labeller id)"""
    a, b = _originals(pass_a), _originals(pass_b)
    if set(a.index) != set(b.index):
        raise ValueError(f"{round_name}: pass a and pass b cover different cases")
    by_case = decisions.set_index("case_id")
    if not by_case.index.is_unique:
        raise ValueError(f"{round_name}: the founder decisions list a case more than once")
    outside = sorted(set(by_case.index) - set(a.index))
    if outside:
        raise ValueError(f"{round_name}: founder decisions for case(s) outside the round, e.g. {outside[:3]}")
    rows, undecided = [], []
    for case_id in sorted(a.index):
        answer_a, answer_b = a.loc[case_id], b.loc[case_id]
        decision = by_case.loc[case_id] if case_id in by_case.index else None
        base = {"case_id": case_id, "policy_version": policy_version, "sampling_stratum": answer_a.sampling_stratum,
                "inclusion_probability": float(answer_a.inclusion_probability), "is_repeat": False,
                "labelling_round": round_name}
        founder_row = None if decision is None else {
            "label": decision.founder_label, "reason_code": FOUNDER_REASON_CODE, "reason": decision.founder_note,
            "labeller_id": LABELLER_FOUNDER, "labelled_at": decision.reviewed_at,
            "adjudication_status": "founder_adjudicated"}
        if answer_a.label != answer_b.label:
            if decision is None or decision.decision != "adjudicated":
                undecided.append(case_id)
                continue
            rows.append({**base, **founder_row})
        elif decision is None:
            rows.append({**base, "label": answer_a.label, "reason_code": answer_a.reason_code, "reason": answer_a.reason,
                         "labeller_id": LABELLER_AGREED, "labelled_at": max(answer_a.labelled_at, answer_b.labelled_at),
                         "adjudication_status": "model_agreed"})
        elif decision.decision == "confirmed":
            rows.append({**base, "label": answer_a.label, "reason_code": answer_a.reason_code, "reason": answer_a.reason,
                         "labeller_id": LABELLER_FOUNDER, "labelled_at": decision.reviewed_at,
                         "adjudication_status": "founder_confirmed"})
        elif decision.decision == "overturned":
            rows.append({**base, **founder_row})
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"{round_name}: case {case_id} has decision {decision.decision!r} but the passes agree")
    if undecided:
        raise UndecidedDisagreements(round_name, undecided)
    for frame in (pass_a, pass_b):
        for r in frame[frame.is_repeat].sort_values("case_id", kind="mergesort").itertuples(index=False):
            rows.append({"case_id": r.case_id, "label": r.label, "reason_code": r.reason_code, "reason": r.reason,
                         "labeller_id": r.labeller_id, "labelled_at": r.labelled_at, "policy_version": policy_version,
                         "sampling_stratum": r.sampling_stratum, "inclusion_probability": float(r.inclusion_probability),
                         "adjudication_status": "blind_repeat", "is_repeat": True, "labelling_round": round_name})
    labels = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=list(LABEL_COLUMNS))
    for i, record in enumerate(labels.to_dict("records")):
        try:
            Label.model_validate(record)
        except Exception as exc:  # pydantic.ValidationError, re-raised with the row
            raise ValueError(f"{round_name}: assembled label row {i} ({record['case_id']}) is invalid: {exc}") from exc
    return labels


def write_founder_digest(rp: RoundPaths, issued_sha256: str) -> str:
    comments = [
        f"The founder's {rp.round_name} review: the returned workbook, the review manifest (the workbook was issued",
        f"with sha256 {issued_sha256}) and the normalised founder decisions. Committed before labels.csv is",
        f"written, so the adjudications behind these {LABEL_DISCLOSURE} labels cannot change silently.",
    ]
    return write_digest_file(rp.founder_digest, [rp.workbook, rp.review_manifest, rp.founder_decisions], comments)
```

```python
def cmd_import_founder_review(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    from ucc_ml import labeling
    from ucc_ml.config import load_config
    from ucc_ml.provenance import read_json

    cfg = load_config(ns.config)
    rp = labeling.round_paths(cfg, ns.round)
    manifest = read_json(rp.review_manifest)
    pass_a, pass_b = labeling.read_pass_file(rp.pass_file("a")), labeling.read_pass_file(rp.pass_file("b"))
    try:
        rows = labeling.read_founder_workbook(rp.workbook)
        decisions, summary = labeling.import_founder_review(rows, manifest, pass_a, pass_b,
                                                            labeling.file_mtime_iso(rp.workbook))
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(f"REJECTED: {exc}")
        return 1
    labeling.write_csv(decisions, rp.founder_decisions)
    labeling.write_founder_digest(rp, manifest["workbook_sha256_issued"])
    disagreements, audit = summary["disagreements"], summary["audit"]
    print(f"disagreements: {disagreements['decided']}/{disagreements['n']} decided")
    print(f"audit: {audit['n_audited']}/{audit['n_selected']} reviewed, confirmed={audit['n_confirmed']} "
          f"overturned={audit['n_overturned']} agreement_rate={audit['agreement_rate']}")
    print(f"wrote {rp.founder_decisions} and {rp.founder_digest}")
    if disagreements["undecided"]:
        print(f"INCOMPLETE: {disagreements['undecided']} disagreement(s) still have no founder decision; "
              "validate-labels will refuse")
        return 1
    return 0


# --- cmd_<name> functions are added above this line by later tasks; each imports its implementation lazily ---
```

```bash
labeller founder); every hidden repeat in each pass -> blind_repeat with that pass's answer. An
undecided disagreement raises UndecidedDisagreements, so no pending row can ever be produced, and
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `ml/src/ucc_ml/labeling.py` (append the labels / manifest / report section)
- Modify: `ml/src/ucc_ml/cli.py` (register `validate-labels` at the two K11 markers)
- Create: `docs/data/ml/labels_v1.sha256` (generated by `validate-labels`, committed)
- Test: `ml/tests/test_validate_labels.py`
- Generated, ignored: `ml/data/labels/v1/labels.csv`, `ml/data/labels/v1/labels_manifest.json`, `ml/data/pilot/v1/pilot_report.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 18's `assemble_round_labels`, `UndecidedDisagreements`, `read_founder_decisions`, `founder_summary`; Task 17's `agreement_report`; Task 16's `read_pass_file`; Task 13's `round_paths`, `write_csv`, `read_exact_csv`, `parse_bool_column`, `write_digest_file`; `ucc_ml.contracts.{ADJUDICATION_STATUSES, LABEL_COLUMNS, LABELS, LABELLING_ROUNDS, STRATA, LABEL_DISCLOSURE, check_column, is_sha256_hex}`; `ucc_ml.config.{load_config, artefact_paths}` (`labels_csv`, `labels_manifest`, `public_data_dir`); `ucc_ml.provenance.{finite_or_none, read_json, write_json, sha256_file, utc_now_iso, git_head}`.
- Produces (`ucc_ml.labeling`) — Plan B reads `labels.csv` only through `read_labels` and `labels_manifest.json` through `provenance.read_json`:
  - `build_labels(round_frames: Mapping[str, pandas.DataFrame]) -> pandas.DataFrame` — `pilot_v1` rows then `main_v1` rows; refuses a case labelled in two rounds, a repeat without its original, a doubled repeat, or rows whose `labelling_round` disagrees with their key.
  - `read_labels(path: Path) -> pandas.DataFrame` — the ONLY reader of `labels.csv` (contract K7): exact `LABEL_COLUMNS`; `case_id` sha256 hex, `label` ∈ `LABELS`, `adjudication_status` ∈ the four K3 statuses, `labelling_round` ∈ `LABELLING_ROUNDS`, `sampling_stratum` ∈ `STRATA`, non-blank `reason`, `inclusion_probability` in (0, 1], `is_repeat` `True`/`False` and consistent with `blind_repeat`; returns `is_repeat` as bool, `inclusion_probability` as float, everything else as str; raises `ValueError` naming the file, column and offending values.
  - `labels_manifest(labels, labels_sha256: str, agreements: Mapping[str, dict], founder: Mapping[str, dict], policy_version: str, extra: Mapping | None = None) -> dict` — K3 keys `policy_version, disclosure, labels_sha256, rows, counts_by_status {status: n}, counts_by_round {round: n}, pass_agreement {round: {n, agreed, rate}}, founder_audit {round: {n_selected, n_audited, n_confirmed, n_overturned, agreement_rate}}, repeat_consistency {pass_a: {n, consistent, rate}, pass_b: {…}}` plus `rounds, cases_by_round, counts_by_label, counts_by_round_and_status, founder_disagreements, repeat_consistency_by_round` and the `extra` keys (`created_at, git_head, config_sha256, inputs_sha256`).
  - `round_report(labels, agreement: dict, founder: dict, round_name: str) -> dict` — K13.1: `labelability {stratum: {n, insufficient, insufficient_share}}`, `relevant_prevalence {stratum: {n, relevant, relevant_share, resolved, relevant_share_of_resolved}}`, `pass_agreement`, `founder_audit`, `founder_disagreements`, `repeat_consistency`, `counts_by_status`, `disclosure`.
  - `load_round_for_validation(rp: RoundPaths, policy_version: str) -> tuple[pandas.DataFrame, dict, dict]`; `write_labels_digest(public_data_dir, labels_csv, manifest_path, manifest) -> Path`; `RESOLVED_LABELS = ("RELEVANT", "NOT_RELEVANT")`.
  - CLI `validate-labels --config …` → merges every round whose passes were imported (`pilot_v1` required, `main_v1` once it exists) into `labels.csv` + `labels_manifest.json`, writes `pilot_report.json` / `main_report.json` and `docs/data/ml/labels_v1.sha256`; exit 1 `REFUSED` (writing nothing) while any disagreement lacks a founder decision or a round's review / decisions file is missing.

```python
"""validate-labels: one labels.csv for every round (K3), read_labels (K7), the manifest and the round report."""
import json

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from conftest import imported_pilot_repo, reviewed_pilot_repo

A, B, C, D, E = ("a" * 64, "b" * 64, "c" * 64, "d" * 64, "e" * 64)
CODES = {"RELEVANT": "R_BORROWER_TRADE_WORD", "NOT_RELEVANT": "N_OTHER_INDUSTRY_EXPLICIT",
         "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE": "I_GENERIC_NAME"}
LABELLERS = {"model_agreed": "claude_blind_pass_a+claude_blind_pass_b", "founder_confirmed": "founder",
             "founder_adjudicated": "founder", "blind_repeat": "claude_blind_pass_a"}


def _row(case_id, status="model_agreed", round_name="pilot_v1", label="RELEVANT", stratum="CO:accepted", labeller=None):
    return {"case_id": case_id, "label": label,
            "reason_code": "ADJUDICATED" if status == "founder_adjudicated" else CODES[label],
            "reason": "the deciding words", "labeller_id": labeller or LABELLERS[status],
            "labelled_at": "2026-09-12T10:00:00Z", "policy_version": "label_policy_v1", "sampling_stratum": stratum,
            "inclusion_probability": 0.25, "adjudication_status": status, "is_repeat": status == "blind_repeat",
            "labelling_round": round_name}


def _frame(rows):
    from ucc_ml.contracts import LABEL_COLUMNS

    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=list(LABEL_COLUMNS))


PILOT = _frame([_row(A), _row(B, "founder_confirmed", stratum="CT:rejected"),
                _row(C, "founder_adjudicated", label="NOT_RELEVANT", stratum="CT:rejected"),
                _row(A, "blind_repeat"), _row(A, "blind_repeat", labeller="claude_blind_pass_b")])
MAIN = _frame([_row(D, round_name="main_v1", label="INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE", stratum="CO:rejected"),
               _row(E, "founder_adjudicated", round_name="main_v1", stratum="CO:rejected")])


def test_build_write_and_read_labels_round_trip(tmp_path):
    from ucc_ml.contracts import LABEL_COLUMNS, Label
    from ucc_ml.labeling import build_labels, read_labels, write_csv

    labels = build_labels({"main_v1": MAIN, "pilot_v1": PILOT})
    assert labels.labelling_round.tolist() == ["pilot_v1"] * 5 + ["main_v1"] * 2
    write_csv(labels, tmp_path / "labels.csv")
    assert (tmp_path / "labels.csv").read_text().splitlines()[0] == ",".join(LABEL_COLUMNS)
    back = read_labels(tmp_path / "labels.csv")
    assert list(back.columns) == list(LABEL_COLUMNS)
    assert back.is_repeat.dtype == bool and back.inclusion_probability.dtype == float
    assert back.is_repeat.tolist() == [False, False, False, True, True, False, False]
    assert back.case_id.tolist() == labels.case_id.tolist()
    for record in back.to_dict("records"):
        Label.model_validate(record)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("frames, message", [
    ({"pilot_v1": PILOT, "main_v1": pd.concat([MAIN, _frame([_row(A, round_name="main_v1")])])}, "labelled more than once"),
    ({"pilot_v1": PILOT.iloc[1:]}, "without an original row"),
    ({"pilot_v1": pd.concat([PILOT, PILOT.tail(1)])}, "repeats a blind_repeat row"),
    ({"pilot_v2": PILOT}, "unknown labelling round"),
    ({"pilot_v1": MAIN}, "carry labelling_round"),
])
def test_build_labels_refuses_inconsistent_rounds(frames, message):
    from ucc_ml.labeling import build_labels

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message):
        build_labels(frames)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("column, value, message", [
    ("label", "MAYBE", "column 'label'.*'MAYBE'"),
    ("adjudication_status", "pending", "column 'adjudication_status'.*'pending'"),
    ("adjudication_status", "adjudicated", "column 'adjudication_status'.*'adjudicated'"),
    ("labelling_round", "pilot_v2", "column 'labelling_round'.*'pilot_v2'"),
    ("is_repeat", "yes", "column 'is_repeat'.*'yes'"),
    ("case_id", "nope", "column 'case_id'.*'nope'"),
    ("inclusion_probability", "0", "column 'inclusion_probability'.*'0'"),
    ("sampling_stratum", "CO|accepted", r"column 'sampling_stratum'.*'CO\|accepted'"),
    ("is_repeat", "True", "disagrees with adjudication_status"),
])
def test_read_labels_names_file_column_and_values(tmp_path, column, value, message):
    from ucc_ml.labeling import build_labels, read_labels, write_csv

    labels = build_labels({"pilot_v1": PILOT}).astype({"is_repeat": object, "inclusion_probability": object})
    labels.loc[0, column] = value
    write_csv(labels, tmp_path / "labels.csv")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message) as exc:
        read_labels(tmp_path / "labels.csv")
    assert "labels.csv" in str(exc.value)


AGREEMENT = {"pass_agreement": {"n": 3, "agreed": 2, "rate": 2 / 3, "by_stratum": {}},
             "repeat_consistency": {"pass_a": {"n": 1, "consistent": 1, "rate": 1.0},
                                    "pass_b": {"n": 1, "consistent": 0, "rate": 0.0}}}
FOUNDER = {"disagreements": {"n": 1, "decided": 1, "undecided": 0},
           "audit": {"n_selected": 2, "n_audited": 2, "n_confirmed": 1, "n_overturned": 1, "agreement_rate": 0.5}}


def test_labels_manifest_carries_the_k3_statistics():
    from ucc_ml.labeling import build_labels, labels_manifest

    labels = build_labels({"pilot_v1": PILOT, "main_v1": MAIN})
    manifest = labels_manifest(labels, "f" * 64, {"pilot_v1": AGREEMENT, "main_v1": AGREEMENT},
                               {"pilot_v1": FOUNDER, "main_v1": FOUNDER}, "label_policy_v1", extra={"git_head": None})
    for key in ("policy_version", "disclosure", "labels_sha256", "rows", "counts_by_status", "counts_by_round",
                "pass_agreement", "founder_audit", "repeat_consistency"):
        assert key in manifest, key
    assert manifest["disclosure"] == "model-labelled, founder-adjudicated" and manifest["rows"] == 7
    assert manifest["counts_by_status"] == {"model_agreed": 2, "founder_confirmed": 1, "founder_adjudicated": 2,
                                            "blind_repeat": 2}
    assert manifest["counts_by_round"] == {"pilot_v1": 5, "main_v1": 2} and manifest["cases_by_round"] == {"pilot_v1": 3, "main_v1": 2}
    assert manifest["pass_agreement"]["main_v1"] == {"n": 3, "agreed": 2, "rate": 2 / 3}
    assert manifest["founder_audit"]["pilot_v1"]["agreement_rate"] == 0.5
    assert manifest["repeat_consistency"] == {"pass_a": {"n": 2, "consistent": 2, "rate": 1.0},
                                              "pass_b": {"n": 2, "consistent": 0, "rate": 0.0}}
    assert manifest["git_head"] is None
    json.dumps(manifest, allow_nan=False)


def test_round_report_is_k13_1():
    from ucc_ml.labeling import build_labels, round_report

    report = round_report(build_labels({"pilot_v1": PILOT, "main_v1": MAIN}), AGREEMENT, FOUNDER, "pilot_v1")
    assert report["cases"] == 3 and report["disclosure"] == "model-labelled, founder-adjudicated"
    assert report["counts_by_status"] == {"model_agreed": 1, "founder_confirmed": 1, "founder_adjudicated": 1, "blind_repeat": 2}
    assert report["labelability"]["CT:rejected"] == {"n": 2, "insufficient": 0, "insufficient_share": 0.0}
    assert report["relevant_prevalence"]["CT:rejected"] == {"n": 2, "relevant": 1, "relevant_share": 0.5,
                                                            "resolved": 2, "relevant_share_of_resolved": 0.5}
    assert report["labelability"]["CO:rejected"] == {"n": 0, "insufficient": 0, "insufficient_share": None}
    assert report["founder_audit"] == FOUNDER["audit"] and report["repeat_consistency"] == AGREEMENT["repeat_consistency"]
    json.dumps(report, allow_nan=False)


def test_cli_validate_labels_writes_labels_manifest_report_and_digest(tmp_path, capsys):
    import hashlib

    from ucc_ml.cli import main
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.labeling import read_labels

    cfg = reviewed_pilot_repo(tmp_path)
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["validate-labels", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "pilot_v1: cases=40" in out and "model-labelled, founder-adjudicated" in out
    paths = artefact_paths(load_config(cfg))
    labels = read_labels(paths.labels_csv)
    counts = labels.adjudication_status.value_counts().to_dict()
    assert counts == {"model_agreed": 35, "founder_adjudicated": 4, "founder_confirmed": 1, "blind_repeat": 10}
    assert set(labels.labelling_round) == {"pilot_v1"} and len(labels) == 50
    manifest = json.loads(paths.labels_manifest.read_text())
    assert manifest["labels_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(paths.labels_csv.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert manifest["counts_by_status"]["blind_repeat"] == 10 and manifest["rounds"] == ["pilot_v1"]
    assert manifest["founder_audit"]["pilot_v1"]["n_audited"] == 2
    assert set(manifest["inputs_sha256"]) == {"ml/data/labels/v1/passes/pass_a_pilot_v1.csv",
                                              "ml/data/labels/v1/passes/pass_b_pilot_v1.csv",
                                              "ml/data/labels/v1/founder_review_pilot_v1.json",
                                              "ml/data/labels/v1/raw/founder_decisions_pilot_v1.csv"}
    report = json.loads((tmp_path / "ml/data/pilot/v1/pilot_report.json").read_text())
    assert {"labelability", "relevant_prevalence", "pass_agreement", "founder_audit", "repeat_consistency"} <= set(report)
    digest = (paths.public_data_dir / "labels_v1.sha256").read_text().splitlines()
    assert digest[0] == f"{manifest['labels_sha256']}  labels.csv" and digest[1].endswith("  labels_manifest.json")
    assert "model-labelled, founder-adjudicated" in digest[2]


def test_cli_validate_labels_refuses_undecided_or_unreviewed_rounds(tmp_path, capsys):
    from ucc_ml.cli import main

    undecided = reviewed_pilot_repo(tmp_path / "undecided", decide_all=False)
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["validate-labels", "--config", str(undecided)]) == 1
    assert "REFUSED: pilot_v1: 1 disagreement(s) have no founder decision" in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert not (tmp_path / "undecided/ml/data/labels/v1/labels.csv").exists()
    unreviewed, _ = imported_pilot_repo(tmp_path / "unreviewed")
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["validate-labels", "--config", str(unreviewed)]) == 1
    assert "founder review manifest" in capsys.readouterr().out
```

```python


# ============================================================================= Task 19
# labels.csv for all rounds, read_labels (contract K7), labels_manifest.json (K3), the round report (K13.1).
from ucc_ml.contracts import ADJUDICATION_STATUSES  # noqa: E402
from ucc_ml.provenance import read_json  # noqa: E402

RESOLVED_LABELS: tuple[str, ...] = ("RELEVANT", "NOT_RELEVANT")


def build_labels(round_frames: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Merge the rounds' K3 rows into one labels.csv frame (pilot_v1 first, then main_v1) and check it."""
    unknown = sorted(set(round_frames) - set(LABELLING_ROUNDS))
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown labelling round(s) {unknown}")
    if not round_frames:
        raise ValueError("no labelling round to merge")
    for round_name, frame in round_frames.items():
        if set(frame.labelling_round) - {round_name}:
            raise ValueError(f"the {round_name} rows carry labelling_round values {sorted(set(frame.labelling_round))}")
    labels = pd.concat([round_frames[r] for r in LABELLING_ROUNDS if r in round_frames], ignore_index=True)
    labels = labels[list(LABEL_COLUMNS)]
    originals, repeats = labels[~labels.is_repeat], labels[labels.is_repeat]
    twice = sorted(set(originals.case_id[originals.case_id.duplicated()]))
    if twice:
        raise ValueError(f"{len(twice)} case_id(s) labelled more than once across rounds, e.g. {twice[:3]}")
    doubled = repeats[repeats.duplicated(["case_id", "labeller_id"])]
    if len(doubled):
        raise ValueError(f"a pass repeats a blind_repeat row for case_id(s) {sorted(set(doubled.case_id))[:3]}")
    orphans = sorted(set(zip(repeats.case_id, repeats.labelling_round))
                     - set(zip(originals.case_id, originals.labelling_round)))
    if orphans:
        raise ValueError(f"blind repeat(s) without an original row in the same round: {orphans[:3]}")
    return labels.reset_index(drop=True)


def _is_probability(text) -> bool:
    try:
        value = float(text)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False
    return 0.0 < value <= 1.0


def read_labels(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """The only reader of labels.csv (contract K7): exact LABEL_COLUMNS, every domain checked, typed.

    is_repeat becomes bool and inclusion_probability float; every other column stays str. A failure raises
    ValueError naming the file, the column and the offending values."""
    path = Path(path)
    df = read_exact_csv(path, LABEL_COLUMNS)
    check_column(path, df, "case_id", is_sha256_hex)
    check_column(path, df, "label", lambda v: v in LABELS)
    check_column(path, df, "adjudication_status", lambda v: v in ADJUDICATION_STATUSES)
    check_column(path, df, "labelling_round", lambda v: v in LABELLING_ROUNDS)
    check_column(path, df, "sampling_stratum", lambda v: v in STRATA)
    check_column(path, df, "reason", lambda v: bool(v.strip()))
    check_column(path, df, "inclusion_probability", _is_probability)
    parse_bool_column(path, df, "is_repeat")
    df["inclusion_probability"] = df.inclusion_probability.astype(float)
    mismatch = df[(df.adjudication_status == "blind_repeat") != df.is_repeat]
    if len(mismatch):
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: column 'is_repeat' disagrees with adjudication_status in {len(mismatch)} row(s), "
                         f"e.g. {mismatch.case_id.tolist()[:3]}")
    return df


def labels_manifest(labels: pd.DataFrame, labels_sha256: str, agreements: Mapping[str, dict],
                    founder: Mapping[str, dict], policy_version: str, extra: Mapping | None = None) -> dict:
    """labels_manifest.json under contract K3 (Plan B reads it; Plan C publishes a whitelist of it)."""
    rounds = [r for r in LABELLING_ROUNDS if r in agreements]
    originals = labels[~labels.is_repeat]
    pooled = {}
    for letter in ("pass_a", "pass_b"):
        n = sum(agreements[r]["repeat_consistency"][letter]["n"] for r in rounds)
        consistent = sum(agreements[r]["repeat_consistency"][letter]["consistent"] for r in rounds)
        pooled[letter] = {"n": int(n), "consistent": int(consistent), "rate": finite_or_none(consistent / n) if n else None}
    manifest = {
        "policy_version": policy_version,
        "disclosure": LABEL_DISCLOSURE,
        "labels_sha256": labels_sha256,
        "rows": int(len(labels)),
        "rounds": rounds,
        "counts_by_status": {s: int((labels.adjudication_status == s).sum()) for s in ADJUDICATION_STATUSES},
        "counts_by_round": {r: int((labels.labelling_round == r).sum()) for r in rounds},
        "cases_by_round": {r: int((originals.labelling_round == r).sum()) for r in rounds},
        "counts_by_label": {label: int((originals.label == label).sum()) for label in LABELS},
        "counts_by_round_and_status": {r: {s: int(((labels.labelling_round == r) & (labels.adjudication_status == s)).sum())
                                           for s in ADJUDICATION_STATUSES} for r in rounds},
        "pass_agreement": {r: {k: agreements[r]["pass_agreement"][k] for k in ("n", "agreed", "rate")} for r in rounds},
        "founder_audit": {r: dict(founder[r]["audit"]) for r in rounds},
        "founder_disagreements": {r: dict(founder[r]["disagreements"]) for r in rounds},
        "repeat_consistency": pooled,
        "repeat_consistency_by_round": {r: agreements[r]["repeat_consistency"] for r in rounds},
    }
    manifest.update(extra or {})
    return manifest


def round_report(labels: pd.DataFrame, agreement: dict, founder: dict, round_name: str) -> dict:
    """Contract K13.1: labelability and RELEVANT prevalence by stratum, pass agreement, founder audit
    agreement and blind-repeat consistency for one round (unweighted within each stratum)."""
    in_round = labels[labels.labelling_round == round_name]
    cases = in_round[~in_round.is_repeat]
    labelability, prevalence = {}, {}
    for stratum in STRATA:
        group = cases[cases.sampling_stratum == stratum]
        n = int(len(group))
        insufficient = int((group.label == "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE").sum())
        relevant = int((group.label == "RELEVANT").sum())
        resolved = int(group.label.isin(RESOLVED_LABELS).sum())
        labelability[stratum] = {"n": n, "insufficient": insufficient,
                                 "insufficient_share": finite_or_none(insufficient / n) if n else None}
        prevalence[stratum] = {"n": n, "relevant": relevant, "relevant_share": finite_or_none(relevant / n) if n else None,
                               "resolved": resolved,
                               "relevant_share_of_resolved": finite_or_none(relevant / resolved) if resolved else None}
    return {
        "round": round_name,
        "disclosure": LABEL_DISCLOSURE,
        "cases": int(len(cases)),
        "counts_by_status": {s: int((in_round.adjudication_status == s).sum()) for s in ADJUDICATION_STATUSES},
        "labelability": labelability,
        "relevant_prevalence": prevalence,
        "pass_agreement": agreement["pass_agreement"],
        "founder_audit": founder["audit"],
        "founder_disagreements": founder["disagreements"],
        "repeat_consistency": agreement["repeat_consistency"],
        "note": "shares are unweighted within each stratum; strata are sampled at different rates, so never pool "
                "them without the design weights",
    }


def load_round_for_validation(rp: RoundPaths, policy_version: str) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict, dict]:
    """(K3 rows, agreement report, founder summary) for a round whose passes, review and decisions all exist.

    Raises FileNotFoundError for a missing input and UndecidedDisagreements while a disagreement is blank."""
    for required, what in ((rp.pass_file("a"), "pass a"), (rp.pass_file("b"), "pass b"),
                           (rp.review_manifest, "the founder review manifest (run review-workbook)"),
                           (rp.founder_decisions, "the founder decisions (run import-founder-review)")):
        if not Path(required).exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"{rp.round_name}: {what} is missing: {required}")
    pass_a, pass_b = read_pass_file(rp.pass_file("a")), read_pass_file(rp.pass_file("b"))
    decisions = read_founder_decisions(rp.founder_decisions)
    labels = assemble_round_labels(pass_a, pass_b, decisions, rp.round_name, policy_version)
    return labels, agreement_report(pass_a, pass_b, rp.round_name), founder_summary(decisions, read_json(rp.review_manifest))


def write_labels_digest(public_data_dir: Path, labels_csv: Path, manifest_path: Path, manifest: dict) -> Path:
    path = Path(public_data_dir) / "labels_v1.sha256"
    status = manifest["counts_by_status"]
    comments = [
        f"labels.csv and labels_manifest.json for rounds {', '.join(manifest['rounds'])}; labels are {LABEL_DISCLOSURE}.",
        "counts_by_status " + ", ".join(f"{s}={status[s]}" for s in ADJUDICATION_STATUSES),
        "pass_agreement " + ", ".join(f"{r}={v['agreed']}/{v['n']}" for r, v in manifest["pass_agreement"].items()),
        "founder_audit " + ", ".join(f"{r}={v['n_confirmed']}/{v['n_audited']}" for r, v in manifest["founder_audit"].items()),
        "repeat_consistency " + ", ".join(f"{p}={v['consistent']}/{v['n']}" for p, v in manifest["repeat_consistency"].items()),
    ]
    write_digest_file(path, [labels_csv, manifest_path], comments)
    return path
```

```python
def cmd_validate_labels(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    from ucc_ml import labeling
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.provenance import git_head, sha256_file, utc_now_iso, write_json

    cfg = load_config(ns.config)
    paths = artefact_paths(cfg)
    rounds = [r for r in ("pilot_v1", "main_v1")
              if any(labeling.round_paths(cfg, r).pass_file(letter).exists() for letter in labeling.PASSES)]
    if "pilot_v1" not in rounds:
        print("REFUSED: the pilot_v1 passes have not been imported (run import-labels --round pilot_v1)")
        return 1
    frames, agreements, founder, inputs = {}, {}, {}, {}
    for round_name in rounds:
        rp = labeling.round_paths(cfg, round_name)
        try:
            frames[round_name], agreements[round_name], founder[round_name] = labeling.load_round_for_validation(
                rp, cfg.version.label_policy_version)
        except (labeling.UndecidedDisagreements, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
            print(f"REFUSED: {exc}; labels.csv was not written")
            return 1
        for used in (rp.pass_file("a"), rp.pass_file("b"), rp.review_manifest, rp.founder_decisions):
            inputs[str(used.relative_to(cfg.repo_root))] = sha256_file(used)
    labels = labeling.build_labels(frames)
    labels_sha = labeling.write_csv(labels, paths.labels_csv)
    manifest = labeling.labels_manifest(
        labels, labels_sha, agreements, founder, cfg.version.label_policy_version,
        extra={"created_at": utc_now_iso(), "git_head": git_head(cfg.repo_root), "config_sha256": cfg.config_sha256,
               "inputs_sha256": inputs})
    write_json(paths.labels_manifest, manifest)
    for round_name in rounds:
        rp = labeling.round_paths(cfg, round_name)
        report = labeling.round_report(labels, agreements[round_name], founder[round_name], round_name)
        write_json(rp.report, report)
        shares = {s: v["insufficient_share"] for s, v in report["labelability"].items()}
        prevalence = {s: v["relevant_share"] for s, v in report["relevant_prevalence"].items()}
        print(f"{round_name}: cases={report['cases']} counts_by_status={report['counts_by_status']}")
        print(f"  INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE share by stratum={shares}")
        print(f"  RELEVANT share by stratum={prevalence}")
        print(f"  pass agreement={report['pass_agreement']['agreed']}/{report['pass_agreement']['n']} "
              f"founder audit={report['founder_audit']['n_confirmed']}/{report['founder_audit']['n_audited']} "
              f"repeat consistency a={report['repeat_consistency']['pass_a']['consistent']}/{report['repeat_consistency']['pass_a']['n']} "
              f"b={report['repeat_consistency']['pass_b']['consistent']}/{report['repeat_consistency']['pass_b']['n']}")
        print(f"  wrote {rp.report}")
    digest = labeling.write_labels_digest(paths.public_data_dir, paths.labels_csv, paths.labels_manifest, manifest)
    print(f"labels rows={manifest['rows']} counts_by_status={manifest['counts_by_status']} "
          f"counts_by_round={manifest['counts_by_round']} ({manifest['disclosure']})")
    print(f"wrote {paths.labels_csv}, {paths.labels_manifest} and {digest}")
    return 0


# --- cmd_<name> functions are added above this line by later tasks; each imports its implementation lazily ---
```

### Task 20: Main-round allocation and the FOUNDER GATE (`propose-main-allocation`, `approve-main-allocation`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `ml/src/ucc_ml/sampling.py` (append the allocation section)
- Modify: `ml/src/ucc_ml/cli.py` (register `approve-main-allocation` and `propose-main-allocation` at the two K11 markers)
- Create: `docs/data/ml/main_v1_allocation.json` (generated by `approve-main-allocation` at the gate, committed)
- Test: `ml/tests/test_main_allocation.py`
- Generated, ignored: `ml/data/main/v1/allocation_proposal.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ucc_ml.sampling.add_stratum`; `ucc_ml.dataset.{read_candidates, read_frame}`; `ucc_ml.splitting.read_splits`; `ucc_ml.contracts.{SPLITS, STRATA}`; `ucc_ml.config.{load_config, artefact_paths}` and `RunConfig.section("budget")["main_round"]` (`{train: 1200, validation: 400, test: 800}`); `ucc_ml.provenance.{sha256_file, utc_now_iso, write_json}`; Task 19's `ml/data/pilot/v1/pilot_report.json` (shown at the gate).
- Produces (`ucc_ml.sampling`):
  - `split_stratum_frame(cases, splits) -> pandas.DataFrame` (`case_id, sampling_stratum, split`; refuses a candidate without a split).
  - `split_stratum_populations(cases, splits, exclude_case_ids: Iterable[str] = ()) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]` — `{split: {stratum: n}}`; `N_h` with nothing excluded, `pool_h` with the pilot excluded.
  - `even_split(total: int) -> dict[str, int]`; `parse_allocation_args(total_args, cell_args, defaults) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[str, dict[str, int]]]` (`--total SPLIT=N`, `--cell SPLIT/STRATUM=N`).
  - `propose_main_allocation(pools, totals, cell_overrides=None) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]` — contract K13.2: each split's total evenly across the four strata, capped by the (split, stratum) pool; a founder cell override replaces a cell and may not exceed its pool.
  - `allocation_document(populations, pools, totals, cell_overrides, allocation) -> dict` (`round, totals, cell_overrides, populations, pools, allocation, capped_cells, total_labels`).
  - CLI `propose-main-allocation [--total SPLIT=N]… [--cell SPLIT/STRATUM=N]…` (prints the table, writes the private proposal) and `approve-main-allocation` with the same flags (writes `docs/data/ml/main_v1_allocation.json` with `approved_by: founder`, `approved_at`, and the sha256 of the candidates, splits, pilot cases and config; refuses once `main_cases.parquet` exists).

Note: the pool of a (split, stratum) cell is its population minus the pilot cases in it. Every pilot case is in train (K12), so validation and test pools equal their populations and the train pools are 50 smaller per stratum. The founder's decision is recorded by a command, not by editing `ml/configs/v1.yaml`: the config keeps the default proposal and `docs/data/ml/main_v1_allocation.json` is the approved allocation `make-main-round` draws.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `ml/tests/test_main_allocation.py`:

```python
"""K13.2: the main round's allocation -- evenly across strata within each split, capped by the non-pilot pool."""
import json

import pytest

from conftest import labelling_repo

TOTALS = {"train": 1200, "validation": 400, "test": 800}


def _pools():
    from ucc_ml.contracts import STRATA

    return {"train": {s: 1000 for s in STRATA},
            "validation": {"CO:accepted": 50, "CO:rejected": 1000, "CT:accepted": 20, "CT:rejected": 1000},
            "test": {s: 1000 for s in STRATA}}


def test_even_split():
    from ucc_ml.sampling import even_split

    assert even_split(1200) == {"CO:accepted": 300, "CO:rejected": 300, "CT:accepted": 300, "CT:rejected": 300}
    assert even_split(402) == {"CO:accepted": 101, "CO:rejected": 101, "CT:accepted": 100, "CT:rejected": 100}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="negative"):
        even_split(-1)


def test_propose_caps_by_pool_and_applies_founder_overrides():
    from ucc_ml.sampling import allocation_document, propose_main_allocation

    allocation = propose_main_allocation(_pools(), TOTALS)
    assert allocation["train"] == {"CO:accepted": 300, "CO:rejected": 300, "CT:accepted": 300, "CT:rejected": 300}
    assert allocation["validation"] == {"CO:accepted": 50, "CO:rejected": 100, "CT:accepted": 20, "CT:rejected": 100}
    assert allocation["test"] == {"CO:accepted": 200, "CO:rejected": 200, "CT:accepted": 200, "CT:rejected": 200}
    overridden = propose_main_allocation(_pools(), TOTALS, {"test": {"CT:accepted": 150}})
    assert overridden["test"]["CT:accepted"] == 150 and overridden["test"]["CO:accepted"] == 200
    document = allocation_document(_pools(), _pools(), TOTALS, {}, allocation)
    assert document["total_labels"] == 1200 + 270 + 800
    assert document["capped_cells"] == [{"split": "validation", "stratum": "CO:accepted", "wanted": 100, "pool": 50},
                                        {"split": "validation", "stratum": "CT:accepted", "wanted": 100, "pool": 20}]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("totals, overrides, message", [
    ({"train": 1, "validation": 1}, None, "exactly"),
    (TOTALS, {"holdout": {"CO:accepted": 1}}, "unknown split"),
    (TOTALS, {"test": {"CO|accepted": 1}}, "unknown stratum"),
    (TOTALS, {"validation": {"CT:accepted": 21}}, "exceeds the pool"),
    (TOTALS, {"test": {"CT:accepted": -1}}, "negative"),
])
def test_propose_refuses_bad_budgets(totals, overrides, message):
    from ucc_ml.sampling import propose_main_allocation

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message):
        propose_main_allocation(_pools(), totals, overrides)


def test_parse_allocation_args():
    from ucc_ml.sampling import parse_allocation_args

    totals, cells = parse_allocation_args(["test=600"], ["test/CT:accepted=150", "validation/CO:rejected=90"], TOTALS)
    assert totals == {"train": 1200, "validation": 400, "test": 600}
    assert cells == {"test": {"CT:accepted": 150}, "validation": {"CO:rejected": 90}}
    for bad_totals, bad_cells in ((["holdout=5"], []), (["test=many"], []), ([], ["test:CT:accepted=1"]), ([], ["test/CT=1"])):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="must look like"):
            parse_allocation_args(bad_totals, bad_cells, TOTALS)


def test_populations_and_pools_exclude_only_the_pilot(tmp_path):
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.contracts import SPLITS, STRATA
    from ucc_ml.dataset import read_candidates, read_frame
    from ucc_ml.sampling import split_stratum_populations
    from ucc_ml.splitting import read_splits

    paths = artefact_paths(load_config(labelling_repo(tmp_path)))
    cases, splits = read_candidates(paths.candidates_parquet), read_splits(paths.splits_parquet)
    pilot = read_frame(paths.pilot_cases)
    populations = split_stratum_populations(cases, splits)
    pools = split_stratum_populations(cases, splits, exclude_case_ids=pilot.case_id)
    assert sum(populations[s][h] for s in SPLITS for h in STRATA) == len(cases) == 120
    assert all(pools["train"][h] == populations["train"][h] - 10 for h in STRATA)     # 10 pilot cases per stratum, all in train
    assert all(pools[s] == populations[s] for s in ("validation", "test"))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="have no split"):
        split_stratum_populations(cases, splits.iloc[1:])


def test_cli_propose_and_approve_main_allocation(tmp_path, capsys):
    from ucc_ml.cli import main

    cfg = labelling_repo(tmp_path)
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["propose-main-allocation", "--config", str(cfg), "--total", "train=8", "--total", "validation=4",
                 "--total", "test=4"]) == 0
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "total_labels=16" in out and "a proposal only" in out
    assert not (tmp_path / "docs/data/ml/main_v1_allocation.json").exists()
    proposal = json.loads((tmp_path / "ml/data/main/v1/allocation_proposal.json").read_text())
    assert proposal["allocation"]["train"] == {"CO:accepted": 2, "CO:rejected": 2, "CT:accepted": 2, "CT:rejected": 2}
    assert "approved_by" not in proposal
    assert main(["approve-main-allocation", "--config", str(cfg), "--total", "train=8", "--total", "validation=4",
                 "--total", "test=4", "--cell", "test/CT:accepted=2"]) == 0
    approved = json.loads((tmp_path / "docs/data/ml/main_v1_allocation.json").read_text())
    assert approved["approved_by"] == "founder" and approved["allocation"]["test"]["CT:accepted"] == 2
    assert approved["cell_overrides"] == {"test": {"CT:accepted": 2}} and approved["total_labels"] == 17
    assert len(approved["splits_sha256"]) == 64 and len(approved["pilot_cases_sha256"]) == 64
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["approve-main-allocation", "--config", str(cfg), "--cell", "test/CT:accepted=99999"]) == 1
    assert "REJECTED" in capsys.readouterr().out
```

```python


# ============================================================================= Task 20
# The main labelling round's allocation (contract K13.2): populations, pools, the proposal, the approval.
from ucc_ml.contracts import SPLITS  # noqa: E402


def split_stratum_frame(cases: pd.DataFrame, splits: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """case_id, sampling_stratum and split for every candidate; refuses a candidate without a split."""
    merged = add_stratum(cases[["case_id", "region", "baseline_qualifies"]]).merge(
        splits[["case_id", "split"]], on="case_id", how="left", validate="one_to_one")
    missing = merged.split.isna()
    if missing.any():
        raise ValueError(f"{int(missing.sum())} candidate(s) have no split, e.g. {merged.case_id[missing].tolist()[:3]}")
    return merged[["case_id", "sampling_stratum", "split"]]


def split_stratum_populations(cases: pd.DataFrame, splits: pd.DataFrame,
                              exclude_case_ids: Iterable[str] = ()) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
    """{split: {stratum: count}} over every candidate minus `exclude_case_ids`.

    With nothing excluded these are the N_h of the main round; with the pilot excluded they are its pool_h."""
    frame = split_stratum_frame(cases, splits)
    frame = frame[~frame.case_id.isin(set(exclude_case_ids))]
    counts = frame.groupby(["split", "sampling_stratum"]).size()
    return {split: {stratum: int(counts.get((split, stratum), 0)) for stratum in STRATA} for split in SPLITS}


def even_split(total: int) -> dict[str, int]:
    """`total` spread evenly over the four strata in STRATA order; a remainder goes to the first strata."""
    if int(total) < 0:
        raise ValueError(f"a budget total cannot be negative: {total}")
    base, remainder = divmod(int(total), len(STRATA))
    return {stratum: base + (1 if i < remainder else 0) for i, stratum in enumerate(STRATA)}


def parse_allocation_args(total_args: Iterable[str], cell_args: Iterable[str],
                          defaults: Mapping[str, int]) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[str, dict[str, int]]]:
    """`--total SPLIT=N` and `--cell SPLIT/STRATUM=N` over the config's default split totals."""
    totals = {split: int(defaults[split]) for split in SPLITS}
    for arg in total_args:
        name, _, value = arg.partition("=")
        if name not in SPLITS or not value.strip().lstrip("-").isdigit():
            raise ValueError(f"--total must look like train=1200, got {arg!r}")
        totals[name] = int(value)
    cells: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for arg in cell_args:
        target, _, value = arg.partition("=")
        split, _, stratum = target.partition("/")
        if split not in SPLITS or stratum not in STRATA or not value.strip().lstrip("-").isdigit():
            raise ValueError(f"--cell must look like test/CT:accepted=150, got {arg!r}")
        cells.setdefault(split, {})[stratum] = int(value)
    return totals, cells


def propose_main_allocation(pools: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, int]], totals: Mapping[str, int],
                            cell_overrides: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, int]] | None = None) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
    """n_h per (split, stratum): each split's total evenly across the strata, capped by the pool; a founder
    cell override replaces the computed value (it may not exceed the pool)."""
    if set(totals) != set(SPLITS):
        raise ValueError(f"budget totals must name exactly {list(SPLITS)}, got {sorted(totals)}")
    overrides = cell_overrides or {}
    for split, cells in overrides.items():
        if split not in SPLITS:
            raise ValueError(f"cell override for unknown split {split!r}")
        for stratum, n in cells.items():
            if stratum not in STRATA:
                raise ValueError(f"cell override for unknown stratum {stratum!r}")
            if int(n) < 0:
                raise ValueError(f"cell override {split}/{stratum}={n} is negative")
            if int(n) > pools[split][stratum]:
                raise ValueError(f"cell override {split}/{stratum}={n} exceeds the pool of {pools[split][stratum]}")
    allocation = {}
    for split in SPLITS:
        wanted = even_split(totals[split])
        allocation[split] = {stratum: int(overrides.get(split, {}).get(stratum, min(wanted[stratum], pools[split][stratum])))
                             for stratum in STRATA}
    return allocation


def allocation_document(populations: Mapping, pools: Mapping, totals: Mapping[str, int],
                        cell_overrides: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, int]] | None, allocation: Mapping) -> dict:
    overrides = cell_overrides or {}
    capped = [{"split": split, "stratum": stratum, "wanted": even_split(totals[split])[stratum], "pool": pools[split][stratum]}
              for split in SPLITS for stratum in STRATA
              if stratum not in overrides.get(split, {}) and allocation[split][stratum] < even_split(totals[split])[stratum]]
    return {
        "round": "main_v1",
        "totals": {split: int(totals[split]) for split in SPLITS},
        "cell_overrides": {split: {s: int(n) for s, n in cells.items()} for split, cells in overrides.items()},
        "populations": {split: dict(populations[split]) for split in SPLITS},
        "pools": {split: dict(pools[split]) for split in SPLITS},
        "allocation": {split: dict(allocation[split]) for split in SPLITS},
        "capped_cells": capped,
        "total_labels": int(sum(n for cells in allocation.values() for n in cells.values())),
    }
```

```python
def _main_round_allocation(ns: argparse.Namespace):
    from ucc_ml import dataset, sampling
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.provenance import sha256_file
    from ucc_ml.splitting import read_splits

    cfg = load_config(ns.config)
    paths = artefact_paths(cfg)
    cases = dataset.read_candidates(paths.candidates_parquet)
    splits = read_splits(paths.splits_parquet)
    pilot_ids = dataset.read_frame(paths.pilot_cases).case_id.tolist()
    totals, cells = sampling.parse_allocation_args(ns.total, ns.cell, cfg.section("budget")["main_round"])
    populations = sampling.split_stratum_populations(cases, splits)
    pools = sampling.split_stratum_populations(cases, splits, exclude_case_ids=pilot_ids)
    allocation = sampling.propose_main_allocation(pools, totals, cells)
    document = sampling.allocation_document(populations, pools, totals, cells, allocation)
    document.update({"candidates_sha256": sha256_file(paths.candidates_parquet),
                     "splits_sha256": sha256_file(paths.splits_parquet),
                     "pilot_cases_sha256": sha256_file(paths.pilot_cases), "config_sha256": cfg.config_sha256})
    return cfg, paths, document


def _print_allocation(document: dict) -> None:
    print(f"{'split':<11}{'stratum':<13}{'N_h':>9}{'pool_h':>9}{'n_h':>7}")
    for split, cells in document["allocation"].items():
        for stratum, n in cells.items():
            print(f"{split:<11}{stratum:<13}{document['populations'][split][stratum]:>9}"
                  f"{document['pools'][split][stratum]:>9}{n:>7}")
    print(f"total_labels={document['total_labels']} capped_cells={len(document['capped_cells'])}")


def cmd_approve_main_allocation(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    from ucc_ml.provenance import utc_now_iso, write_json

    try:
        cfg, paths, document = _main_round_allocation(ns)
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(f"REJECTED: {exc}")
        return 1
    if paths.main_cases.exists():
        print(f"REFUSED: {paths.main_cases} already exists; the main round was drawn from an earlier approval")
        return 1
    document.update({"approved_by": "founder", "approved_at": utc_now_iso()})
    out = paths.public_data_dir / "main_v1_allocation.json"
    write_json(out, document)
    _print_allocation(document)
    print(f"wrote {out} (approved: make-main-round draws exactly this allocation)")
    return 0


def cmd_propose_main_allocation(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    from ucc_ml.provenance import utc_now_iso, write_json

    try:
        cfg, _, document = _main_round_allocation(ns)
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(f"REJECTED: {exc}")
        return 1
    document["created_at"] = utc_now_iso()
    out = cfg.path("main_round_dir") / "allocation_proposal.json"
    write_json(out, document)
    _print_allocation(document)
    print(f"wrote {out} (a proposal only; nothing is drawn until the founder approves it)")
    return 0


# --- cmd_<name> functions are added above this line by later tasks; each imports its implementation lazily ---
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 20's `split_stratum_frame`, `docs/data/ml/main_v1_allocation.json`; Task 9's `draw_stratified`, `SAMPLE_COLUMNS`; `ucc_ml.contracts.{SPLITS, STRATA}`; `ucc_ml.dataset.{read_candidates, read_frame, write_frame}`; `ucc_ml.splitting.read_splits`; `ucc_ml.labeling.{round_paths, labels_exist}` (Task 13); `ucc_ml.config.artefact_paths` (`main_cases`, `main_manifest`, `public_data_dir`); conftest `labelling_repo`, `reviewed_pilot_repo`, `run_blind_passes`, `fill_founder_workbook`, `founder_answers`.
- Produces (`ucc_ml.sampling`):
  - `MAIN_ROUND_DESIGN_COLUMNS = ("split", "sampling_stratum", "N_h", "pool_h", "n_h", "inclusion_probability", "draw_rank")`.
  - `make_main_round(cases, splits, pilot_case_ids: Iterable[str], allocation: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, int]], seed: int, purpose: str = "main_v1") -> pandas.DataFrame` — per (split, stratum) a seeded `draw_stratified` with `purpose = "main_v1:<split>"`, `N_h` = the split-stratum population, `pool_h` = `N_h` minus pilot cases, `n_h` from the allocation, `inclusion_probability = n_h / pool_h`, `sampling_stratum` in the K2 colon form; columns `CASE_COLUMNS + MAIN_ROUND_DESIGN_COLUMNS`; never contains a pilot case.
  - CLI `make-main-round --config …` → `main_cases.parquet` + `main_manifest.json` (`{purpose, dataset_version, seed, allocation_sha256, allocation, cells {split: {stratum: {N_h, pool_h, n_h, inclusion_probability}}}, rows, by_split, by_region, case_id_digest, main_cases_sha256, candidates_sha256, splits_sha256, pilot_cases_sha256, created_at, git_head, config_sha256}`); exit 1 `REFUSED` without an approved allocation, when an input changed since approval, or once any main-round label exists.
  - `main_cases.parquet` is the round-cases file `label-blind --round main_v1` reads (it carries every `ROUND_CASE_COLUMNS` column).

```python
"""K13.3: the main round is drawn inside the frozen splits from non-pilot cases, with its design recorded;
K13.4-K13.7: it is pre-registered, labelled blind, reviewed, and merged with the pilot into one labels.csv."""
import json

import pytest

from conftest import fill_founder_workbook, founder_answers, labelling_repo, reviewed_pilot_repo, run_blind_passes

SMALL = ["--total", "train=8", "--total", "validation=4", "--total", "test=4"]


def test_make_main_round_design(tmp_path):
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.contracts import CASE_COLUMNS, stratum_name
    from ucc_ml.dataset import read_candidates, read_frame
    from ucc_ml.sampling import make_main_round, propose_main_allocation, split_stratum_populations
    from ucc_ml.splitting import read_splits

    paths = artefact_paths(load_config(labelling_repo(tmp_path)))
    cases, splits = read_candidates(paths.candidates_parquet), read_splits(paths.splits_parquet)
    pilot_ids = read_frame(paths.pilot_cases).case_id.tolist()
    populations = split_stratum_populations(cases, splits)
    pools = split_stratum_populations(cases, splits, exclude_case_ids=pilot_ids)
    allocation = propose_main_allocation(pools, {"train": 8, "validation": 4, "test": 4})
    main = make_main_round(cases, splits, pilot_ids, allocation, seed=20260912)
    assert list(main.columns) == list(CASE_COLUMNS) + ["split", "sampling_stratum", "N_h", "pool_h", "n_h",
                                                       "inclusion_probability", "draw_rank"]
    assert len(main) == 16 and main.case_id.is_unique and not set(main.case_id) & set(pilot_ids)
    split_of = splits.set_index("case_id").split
    assert all(split_of[c] == s for c, s in zip(main.case_id, main.split))
    for (split, stratum), group in main.groupby(["split", "sampling_stratum"]):
        assert (group.N_h == populations[split][stratum]).all() and (group.pool_h == pools[split][stratum]).all()
        assert (group.n_h == allocation[split][stratum]).all()
        assert group.inclusion_probability.iloc[0] == pytest.approx(allocation[split][stratum] / pools[split][stratum])
        assert all(stratum_name(r, bool(q)) == stratum for r, q in zip(group.region, group.baseline_qualifies))
    train = main[main.split == "train"]
    assert (train.pool_h == train.N_h - 10).all()                 # the pilot is in train's population, not its pool
    held_out = main[main.split != "train"]
    assert (held_out.pool_h == held_out.N_h).all()
    assert main.case_id.tolist() == make_main_round(cases, splits, pilot_ids, allocation, seed=20260912).case_id.tolist()
    assert main.case_id.tolist() != make_main_round(cases, splits, pilot_ids, allocation, seed=1).case_id.tolist()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="asked for"):
        make_main_round(cases, splits, pilot_ids, {"test": {"CO:accepted": 10_000}}, seed=1)


def test_cli_make_main_round_refuses_without_an_approved_allocation(tmp_path, capsys):
    from ucc_ml.cli import main

    cfg = labelling_repo(tmp_path)
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["make-main-round", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 1
    assert "has not approved an allocation" in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert main(["approve-main-allocation", "--config", str(cfg), *SMALL]) == 0
    splits = tmp_path / "ml/data/splits/v1/splits.parquet"
    splits.write_bytes(splits.read_bytes() + b"\0")                        # the splits changed after approval
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["make-main-round", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 1
    assert "splits_sha256" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_main_round_draw_preregistration_and_merge_with_the_pilot(tmp_path, capsys):
    from ucc_ml.cli import main
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.labeling import read_key, read_labels, round_paths

    cfg = reviewed_pilot_repo(tmp_path)
    paths = artefact_paths(load_config(cfg))
    assert main(["approve-main-allocation", "--config", str(cfg), *SMALL]) == 0
    assert main(["make-main-round", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0
    manifest = json.loads(paths.main_manifest.read_text())
    assert manifest["rows"] == 16 and manifest["by_split"] == {"train": 8, "validation": 4, "test": 4}
    assert set(manifest["cells"]) == {"train", "validation", "test"}
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["label-blind", "--config", str(cfg), "--round", "main_v1"]) == 0
    assert "round=main_v1 cases=16 repeats=2 parts=2 chunk_size=15" in capsys.readouterr().out
    rp = round_paths(load_config(cfg), "main_v1")
    lines = rp.preregistration.read_text().splitlines()
    assert [line.split("  ", 1)[1] for line in lines[:5]] == [
        "main_cases.parquet", "main_manifest.json", "queue_key_main_v1.csv",
        "queue_main_v1_part_001.csv", "queue_main_v1_part_002.csv"]
    key = read_key(rp.key)
    run_blind_passes(cfg, "main_v1", flips_in_pass_b=key[~key.is_repeat].queue_case_id.tolist()[:1])
    assert main(["make-main-round", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 1                  # labels exist: the draw is frozen
    for command in (["import-labels", "--round", "main_v1"], ["review-workbook", "--round", "main_v1"]):
        assert main([command[0], "--config", str(cfg), *command[1:]]) == 0
    capsys.readouterr()
    assert main(["validate-labels", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 1                 # main_v1 decisions not imported yet
    assert "REFUSED: main_v1" in capsys.readouterr().out
    fill_founder_workbook(rp.workbook, founder_answers(cfg, "main_v1"))
    assert main(["import-founder-review", "--config", str(cfg), "--round", "main_v1"]) == 0
    assert main(["validate-labels", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0
    labels = read_labels(paths.labels_csv)
    assert labels.labelling_round.value_counts().to_dict() == {"pilot_v1": 50, "main_v1": 20}
    assert not set(labels[labels.labelling_round == "main_v1"].case_id) & set(labels[labels.labelling_round == "pilot_v1"].case_id)
    merged = json.loads(paths.labels_manifest.read_text())
    assert merged["rounds"] == ["pilot_v1", "main_v1"] and merged["cases_by_round"] == {"pilot_v1": 40, "main_v1": 16}
    assert merged["pass_agreement"]["main_v1"]["agreed"] == 15
    assert (tmp_path / "ml/data/main/v1/main_report.json").exists()
```

```python


# ============================================================================= Task 21
# The main round draw (contract K13.3): within the frozen splits, from non-pilot cases only.
MAIN_ROUND_DESIGN_COLUMNS: tuple[str, ...] = ("split",) + tuple(c for c in SAMPLE_COLUMNS if c != "case_id")


def make_main_round(cases: pd.DataFrame, splits: pd.DataFrame, pilot_case_ids: Iterable[str],
                    allocation: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, int]], seed: int, purpose: str = "main_v1") -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A seeded stratified draw inside every (split, stratum): N_h is the split-stratum population, pool_h is
    N_h minus the pilot cases in it, n_h comes from the approved allocation, inclusion_probability = n_h / pool_h.

    Columns: the candidate columns, then split, sampling_stratum, N_h, pool_h, n_h, inclusion_probability,
    draw_rank; rows ordered by split, stratum and draw_rank."""
    pilot = set(pilot_case_ids)
    frame = split_stratum_frame(cases, splits)
    parts = []
    for split in SPLITS:
        wanted = {stratum: int(n) for stratum, n in allocation.get(split, {}).items() if int(n) > 0}
        if not wanted:
            continue
        drawn = draw_stratified(frame[frame.split == split], wanted, seed=seed, purpose=f"{purpose}:{split}",
                                exclude_case_ids=pilot)
        drawn.insert(1, "split", split)
        parts.append(drawn)
    if not parts:
        raise ValueError("the allocation draws no case")
    sample = pd.concat(parts, ignore_index=True)
    if set(sample.case_id) & pilot:
        raise AssertionError("a pilot case was drawn into the main round")
    merged = sample.merge(cases, on="case_id", how="left", validate="one_to_one")
    merged["_order"] = [(SPLITS.index(sp), STRATA.index(st)) for sp, st in zip(merged.split, merged.sampling_stratum)]
    merged = merged.sort_values(["_order", "draw_rank"], kind="mergesort")
    columns = ["case_id"] + [c for c in cases.columns if c != "case_id"] + list(MAIN_ROUND_DESIGN_COLUMNS)
    return merged[columns].reset_index(drop=True)
```

```python
def cmd_make_main_round(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    import hashlib

    from ucc_ml import dataset, labeling, sampling
    from ucc_ml.config import artefact_paths, load_config
    from ucc_ml.contracts import SPLITS
    from ucc_ml.provenance import git_head, read_json, sha256_file, utc_now_iso, write_json
    from ucc_ml.splitting import read_splits

    cfg = load_config(ns.config)
    paths = artefact_paths(cfg)
    approved_path = paths.public_data_dir / "main_v1_allocation.json"
    if not approved_path.exists():
        print(f"REFUSED: {approved_path} does not exist; the Task 20 founder gate has not approved an allocation")
        return 1
    if labeling.labels_exist(labeling.round_paths(cfg, "main_v1")):
        print("REFUSED: main_v1 already has labeller output; the main round is frozen")
        return 1
    approved = read_json(approved_path)
    inputs = {"candidates_sha256": sha256_file(paths.candidates_parquet),
              "splits_sha256": sha256_file(paths.splits_parquet),
              "pilot_cases_sha256": sha256_file(paths.pilot_cases)}
    stale = sorted(k for k, v in inputs.items() if approved.get(k) != v)
    if stale:
        print(f"REFUSED: the approved allocation was computed for different inputs ({', '.join(stale)}); repeat the Task 20 gate")
        return 1
    cases = dataset.read_candidates(paths.candidates_parquet)
    splits = read_splits(paths.splits_parquet)
    pilot_ids = dataset.read_frame(paths.pilot_cases).case_id.tolist()
    main_cases = sampling.make_main_round(cases, splits, pilot_ids, approved["allocation"], cfg.seed)
    sha = dataset.write_frame(main_cases, paths.main_cases)
    cells: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for (split, stratum), group in main_cases.groupby(["split", "sampling_stratum"], sort=False):
        cells.setdefault(split, {})[stratum] = {
            "N_h": int(group.N_h.iloc[0]), "pool_h": int(group.pool_h.iloc[0]), "n_h": int(group.n_h.iloc[0]),
            "inclusion_probability": float(group.inclusion_probability.iloc[0])}
    manifest = {
        "purpose": "main_v1", "dataset_version": cfg.version.dataset_version, "seed": cfg.seed,
        "allocation_sha256": sha256_file(approved_path), "allocation": approved["allocation"], "cells": cells,
        "rows": int(len(main_cases)),
        "by_split": {split: int((main_cases.split == split).sum()) for split in SPLITS},
        "by_region": {k: int(v) for k, v in sorted(main_cases.region.value_counts().items())},
        "case_id_digest": hashlib.sha256(("\n".join(sorted(main_cases.case_id)) + "\n").encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
        "main_cases_sha256": sha, **inputs,
        "created_at": utc_now_iso(), "git_head": git_head(cfg.repo_root), "config_sha256": cfg.config_sha256,
    }
    write_json(paths.main_manifest, manifest)
    print(f"main round rows={manifest['rows']} by_split={manifest['by_split']} by_region={manifest['by_region']}")
    for split, by_stratum in cells.items():
        print(f"  {split}: " + ", ".join(f"{stratum} n_h={v['n_h']} pool_h={v['pool_h']} p={v['inclusion_probability']:.6g}"
                                         for stratum, v in by_stratum.items()))
    print(f"wrote {paths.main_cases} sha256={sha} and {paths.main_manifest}")
    return 0


# --- cmd_<name> functions are added above this line by later tasks; each imports its implementation lazily ---
```

For each part (`NNN` = 001 … 012), exactly as Task 15 Step 8 and `ml/specs/labeller_prompt_v1.md` state (contract K14):

1. Spawn ONE fresh subagent with no tools. Its entire prompt is the text of `ml/data/labels/v1/briefs/brief_main_v1_part_NNN.md`, verbatim; its structured output schema is `ml/data/labels/v1/briefs/labeller_output_schema_v1.json`. Run at most five labeller subagents at the same time.
2. Save its structured output verbatim as `ml/data/labels/v1/raw/structured/labeller_output_main_v1_pass_a_part_NNN.json`.
3. After each agent returns (or after a batch of agents), write the raw CSV of every saved pass-A JSON that has no CSV yet:

```bash
done
```

### Task 24: Main-round agreement, founder review workbook and FOUNDER GATE; `import-founder-review --round main_v1`

**Files:**

- Consumes: Task 23's pass files; Task 17's `review-workbook`; Task 18's `import-founder-review`; config `labelling.founder_audit_per_split_stratum` (10).
- Produces: the main-round agreement report, the founder's review workbook (every disagreement plus 10 agreed rows per split x stratum, up to 120 audit rows), the imported founder decisions, and the committed digest `docs/data/ml/main_v1_founder_review.sha256`.


Expected: `pass agreement: <agreed>/2400 rate=<rate>`, `repeat consistency: pass_a <k>/240, pass_b <k>/240`, `founder review rows=<d + audit> disagreements=<d> audit=<at most 120>`, the three `wrote` paths (exit 0); then the counts, `audit_by_split_stratum` with 10 per cell for `train`, `validation` and `test` (fewer only where a cell has fewer agreed cases), and agreement by stratum with repeat consistency per pass.

- [ ] **Step 2: FOUNDER GATE — hand the main-round review workbook to the founder (STOP here and ask)**

Stop. Tell the founder, in one message:

1. The workbook to review is `./ml/data/labels/v1/founder_review_main_v1.xlsx` (private, gitignored, never published). Its `HOW TO REVIEW` sheet is the instruction; the procedure is the same as the pilot's.
2. The numbers from Step 1: pass agreement (agreed/2400, and by stratum), blind-repeat consistency for each pass (k/240), and the row count (every disagreement plus up to 120 agreed rows, 10 per split x stratum).
3. What to do: choose `founder_label` on every row; write a one-sentence `founder_note` on every disagreement and on every agreed row where the founder's label differs from the two passes; save under the same name; do not add, delete or re-type rows or other columns; no lookups.
4. That validation and test labels decided here set the operating threshold and the final estimate, so the founder decides from the names shown and nothing else (no model output exists yet).

Do not run Step 3 until the founder says the workbook is saved and complete.


On `REJECTED: …` or `INCOMPLETE: …` (exit 1), send the founder the message verbatim, wait for the corrected workbook and re-run this step; never fill a founder cell on the founder's behalf.

```bash
ml: founder review of the main labelling round -- decisions imported, review digest frozen

Contract K13.6. review-workbook compared the two blind main-round passes on all 2,400 cases
(agreement overall and by stratum, blind-repeat consistency for each pass over 240 hidden
repeats) and issued the founder's workbook: every disagreement plus an audit sample of 10 agreed
cases per split x stratum, showing only the names, city/state and both passes' answers. At this
task's gate the founder decided every disagreement and reviewed the audit rows; import-founder-review
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces for Plan B (final): `ml/data/labels/v1/labels.csv` with both rounds (`LABEL_COLUMNS`, the four K3 statuses, `labelling_round ∈ {pilot_v1, main_v1}`), readable only through `ucc_ml.labeling.read_labels`; `ml/data/labels/v1/labels_manifest.json` with the K3 statistics for both rounds; `docs/data/ml/labels_v1.sha256`.

