
Expected: a `pilot_v1:` block (`cases=200`) and a `main_v1:` block (`cases=2400`), each with its INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE and RELEVANT shares by stratum, pass agreement, founder audit and repeat consistency, then `labels rows=3120 counts_by_status={…} counts_by_round={'pilot_v1': 240, 'main_v1': 2880} (model-labelled, founder-adjudicated)` and the `wrote` line (exit 0). `REFUSED: main_v1: …` means Task 24's gate is not finished: return to Task 24 Step 2.

- [ ] **Step 2: Verify the file Plan B will read**

Run:

```bash
./.venv-ml/bin/python - <<'EOF'
from ucc_ml.contracts import RESOLVED_ADJUDICATION_STATUSES
from ucc_ml.labeling import read_labels
from ucc_ml.splitting import read_splits

labels = read_labels("ml/data/labels/v1/labels.csv")
originals = labels[~labels.is_repeat]
print("rows", len(labels), "rounds", labels.labelling_round.value_counts().to_dict())
print("statuses", labels.adjudication_status.value_counts().to_dict())
print("cases in both rounds", len(set(originals[originals.labelling_round == "pilot_v1"].case_id) & set(originals[originals.labelling_round == "main_v1"].case_id)))
kept = originals[originals.adjudication_status.isin(RESOLVED_ADJUDICATION_STATUSES) & originals.label.isin(["RELEVANT", "NOT_RELEVANT"])]
split_of = read_splits("ml/data/splits/v1/splits.parquet").set_index("case_id").split
print("resolved binary by split", kept.case_id.map(split_of).value_counts().to_dict())
print("pilot rows outside train", int((originals[originals.labelling_round == "pilot_v1"].case_id.map(split_of) != "train").sum()))
EOF
cat docs/data/ml/labels_v1.sha256
```

Expected: `rows 3120 rounds {'main_v1': 2880, 'pilot_v1': 240}` at the default allocation; the four statuses with `blind_repeat` equal to 520 (2 passes x 260 hidden repeats); `cases in both rounds 0`; resolved binary counts for `train`, `validation` and `test` (the denominators Plan B's evaluation protocol reports); `pilot rows outside train 0`; then the two-line digest of `labels.csv` and `labels_manifest.json` with its comment lines (disclosure, counts by status, pass agreement and founder audit per round, repeat consistency per pass).

```bash
git add docs/data/ml/labels_v1.sha256

ml: labels v1 complete -- pilot_v1 and main_v1 merged into one labels.csv (model-labelled, founder-adjudicated)

Contract K13.7. validate-labels merged the 200-case pilot and the main labelling round into a
single ml/data/labels/v1/labels.csv with its labels_manifest.json, refusing -- as designed -- until
every disagreement in both rounds carried a founder decision. Every row is one of the four K3
statuses (model_agreed, founder_confirmed, founder_adjudicated, blind_repeat) and names its
labelling_round; no case is labelled in both rounds and every pilot case is in train.
labels_manifest.json carries the disclosure "model-labelled, founder-adjudicated" and the K3
statistics for both rounds: counts by status and by round, pass agreement and founder audit
agreement per round, and blind-repeat consistency for each pass. The main-round report
(ml/data/main/v1/main_report.json) holds labelability and RELEVANT prevalence by stratum.

docs/data/ml/labels_v1.sha256 now commits the digests of the final labels.csv and its manifest,
with the counts in its comment lines. Plan A is complete: Plan B's real-data run (train,
freeze-candidate, the pre-registered single evaluate-final on test, build-release, score-batch)
reads candidates.parquet, splits.parquet, labels.csv and labels_manifest.json through
read_candidates, read_splits, read_labels and artefact_paths.
```

## Self-review (against the contract, the context pack and the Codex plan)

### 1. Spec coverage

| Requirement | Where |
|---|---|
| K1 plan format (Create / Replace in / Append to; `Run:` + fenced commands + `Expected:`; commit by path with the two trailers) | every task; the whole plan materialises through a K1-only applier with no rejected instruction |
| K2 strata `CO:accepted` colon form | Task 3 (`STRATA`, `stratum_name`; pipe form rejected by `Label`), used in Tasks 8–11, 13, 17, 19–21 |
| K3 four statuses, no pending, `labelling_round`, labeller ids, `RESOLVED_ADJUDICATION_STATUSES`, manifest keys | Task 3 (vocabulary, `Label` rules), Task 12 (policy §7), Task 18 (`assemble_round_labels`, `UndecidedDisagreements`), Task 19 (`labels_manifest`, `validate-labels` refusal) |
| K4 whole `v1.yaml` incl. B/C sections, `section()`, no `release_id`, `predictions_dir`, `main_round_dir`, `repo_root = parents[2]` | Task 2 (config, real-config test for every B/C section, duplicate keys refused) |
| K5 `ArtefactPaths` / `artefact_paths` | Task 2 (all 21 values tested); every CLI command from Task 8 on reads its paths through it |
| K6 provenance API | Task 2 (`test_provenance.py`: known vectors, NaN refusal, SHA256SUMS round trip, tamper, missing, unlisted, malformed names) |
| K7 readers with validation | Task 7 `read_candidates`, Task 11 `read_splits`, Task 19 `read_labels` (each with refusal tests naming file, column and values) |
| K8, K9, K10 | owned by Plans B and C; Plan A supplies their inputs (artefact paths, config sections, K3 manifest keys, the sections a synthetic `write_config` must carry) |
| K11 CLI pattern and stable marker | Task 1 (both markers pinned, lazy-import test); Tasks 8, 10, 11, 13, 15–21 register at the markers |
| K12 pilot groups → train | Task 11 (`assign_splits`, audit key `pilot_cases_not_in_train`, tests) |
| K13.1 pilot report | Task 19 (`round_report`, `pilot_report.json`, printed) |
| K13.2 allocation + founder gate | Task 20 (`propose_main_allocation`, `approve-main-allocation`, gate) |
| K13.3 `make-main-round` design | Task 21 (`N_h`, `pool_h`, `n_h`, `inclusion_probability`, colon strata, no pilot case) |
| K13.4 pre-registration before labels | Task 14 (`pilot_v1.sha256`), Task 22 (`main_v1.sha256`); `label-blind` refuses once labels exist |
| K13.5 chunked queues with 10% hidden repeats per chunk + private key | Task 13 (code), Tasks 14 and 22 (runs) |
| K13.6 two passes, import, agreement, founder workbook (every disagreement + 10 per split x stratum), gate | Tasks 15–18 (pilot), Tasks 23–24 (main) |
| K13.7 `validate-labels` merges both rounds, refuses undecided disagreements | Task 19 (code + pilot run), Task 21 (end-to-end test of the merge), Task 25 (real merge) |
| K14 blind labeller protocol and prompt text | Task 12 (`labeller_prompt_v1.md`), Task 15 (briefs, schema, writer, pilot passes), Task 23 (main passes) |
| K15 disclosure literal + label statistics | Task 2 (config refuses another disclosure), Task 3 (`LABEL_DISCLOSURE`), Tasks 13, 16, 18, 19 (every digest comment), Task 19 (manifest and round report) |
| K16 unchanged rulings | Global Constraints; Task 5 (read-only export under `.venv`), Tasks 6–8 (build_scope eligibility, region-scoped `group_id`, reconciliation), `225 passed` checks in Tasks 1, 15, 19, 21, 25 |
| Pack §0.2 no lookups; §0.3 Claude labels, founder reviews, disclosure, 10% repeats; §0.8 prompts only at decision points | Tasks 12, 15, 17, 18, 20, 23, 24; four gates (Tasks 12, 18, 20, 24) |
| Pack §3 eligibility, exclusion counts, duplicates, blank lenders, baseline parity | Tasks 6–8 |
| Pack §4 vendoring with manifest and 30-name parity; founder workbook format | Task 4; Task 17 (`make_workbook_v4` style) |
| Codex §4 completion checks (unique ids, stable digest, both baseline populations, reconciliation, multi-lender sets, join expansion) | Tasks 5–8 |
| Codex §5 labels, reviewer blind to score/baseline/stratum, repeats removed before fitting, 200-case development pilot, 800–1,200 / 250–400 / 500–800 planning budget, `N_h`/`n_h`/inclusion recorded before labelling | Tasks 10, 13–25 (default main round at the upper end of the Codex budget, approved at Task 20) |
| Codex §6 grouped 20/15/65 splits persisted, pilot kept out of test, identical-name audit, split digest with policy version | Task 11 |
| Codex §12 CLI `build-candidates`, `make-pilot`, `freeze-splits`, `validate-labels` | Tasks 8, 10, 11, 19 |

Deliberate deviations, each decided by a higher-precedence source: the Codex `evidence_url_or_reference` / `evidence_date` fields are dropped (pack §0.2); the Codex "second person" is replaced by two blind Claude passes plus the founder (pack §0.3); pilot groups go to train, not merely out of test (K12); `make-pilot --count 200` is `sampling.pilot_per_stratum: 50` over four strata (pack §6). Not covered, carried as a residual risk: Codex §6's grouping of near-duplicate name/address variants beyond an identical `name_clean`.

### 2. Placeholder scan

The assembled plan was searched for `TODO`, `TBD`, "implement later", "fill in", "similar to Task", "add appropriate", "handle edge cases" and `<fill`: the only hit is the placeholder-address literal `'TBD'` inside the copied eligibility list, which is data. Every expected test count is a number collected from a materialised build of this plan. Expected-output lines keep `<…>` or `…` only for values produced by the real data (row counts, hashes, rates, agreement), and each such line says what is checked. No step describes a file edit in prose; founder decisions are recorded by commands (`approve-main-allocation`, `import-founder-review`) or by the policy's checklist.

### 3. Type consistency

The names in "Produces for Plans B and C" and in every task's Interfaces were imported from a materialised build: every promised name exists in `config`, `provenance`, `contracts`, `legacy`, `dataset`, `splitting`, `sampling` and `labeling`; `ArtefactPaths` has exactly the 21 K5 fields; the parser registers the 14 commands the header lists; each cli.py marker occurs once. The signatures in the text match the code: `verify_sha256sums(directory, required=())`, `write_sha256sums(directory, filenames)`, `artefact_paths(cfg)`, `read_candidates(path)`, `read_splits(path)`, `read_labels(path)`, `build_blind_queue(round_cases, round_name, repeat_fraction, seed, chunk_size)`, `assemble_round_labels(pass_a, pass_b, decisions, round_name, policy_version)`, `labels_manifest(labels, labels_sha256, agreements, founder, policy_version, extra=None)`, `make_main_round(cases, splits, pilot_case_ids, allocation, seed, purpose="main_v1")`, `stratum_name(region, baseline_qualifies)`. File names are spelled once, in `artefact_paths` and `RoundPaths`, and `test_round_paths_name_every_file` pins the queue, raw-output (K14) and digest names the prose uses.
